GBP. eyPvi Ab~w`Z A¨vwi÷Uj Zuvi Ô†cv‡qwU·Õ (350 wLªóc~e©vã) MÖ‡š’ ej‡Qb :
Tragedy, then, is an imitation of an action that is serious, complete, and of a certain magnitude; in
language embellished with each kind of artistic ornament, the several kinds being found in separate parts
of the play; in the form of action, not of narrative; through pity and fear effecting the proper purgation of
these emotions.
